     1	# Green Hydrogen Hub Resilience Planning Model
     2
     3	## Formulation choice
     4
     5	This instance is best modeled as a two-stage stochastic mixed-integer program in
     6	extensive form.
     7
     8	- First-stage variables choose the common infrastructure posture for each site:
     9	  whether the site is active, how many electrolyzer modules are installed, how
    10	  many tank modules are installed, and whether polishing capability is enabled.
    11	- Second-stage variables are scenario-specific hourly operating decisions:
    12	  online modules, startups, production, energy use, polishing, inventory,
    13	  deliveries, trips, venting, and shortfalls.
    14
    15	This is stronger and simpler than a decomposition-first approach for the public
    16	instance because the instance is small:
    17
    18	- 4 production sites
    19	- 6 customers
    20	- 24 site-customer lanes
    21	- 6 scenarios
    22	- 12 hours
    23
    24	The only cross-scenario coupling is in the first-stage infrastructure, so the
    25	extensive form remains tractable while preserving exact nonanticipativity.
    26
    27	## Sets and indices
    28
    29	- Sites `s in S`
    30	- Customers `c in C`
    31	- Delivery lanes `l in L`
    32	- Scenarios `w in W`
    33	- Hours `t in T = {0, ..., 11}`
    34
    35	Derived subsets:
    36
    37	- `L(s)` = lanes whose origin is site `s`
    38	- `L(c)` = lanes whose customer is `c`
    39	- `C_hi` = customers requiring polished hydrogen
    40	- `C_lo` = customers that can use unpolished hydrogen
    41
    42	## Public-data assumptions fixed for the model
    43
    44	The brief leaves a few items implicit. I use the least risky interpretation that
    45	matches the supplied fields:
    46
    47	1. Customers with `purity_required_pct >= 99.9` are treated as high-purity and
    48	   must be served from polished hydrogen. The only public customer below that
    49	   threshold is `STEEL_PILOT` at `99.7`, so it may be served by unpolished
    50	   hydrogen.
    51	2. Initial inventory is treated as unpolished hydrogen because the public data
    52	   does not provide any initial polished-stock split.
    53	3. `water_limit_multiplier` scales the site's daily water permit from
    54	   `sites.csv`, because the base permit is given in `m3/day`.
    55	4. `travel_time_multiplier` scales driver-hours consumption but not trip cost,
    56	   because the brief says scenario lane rows tighten movement and travel burden;
    57	   it does not say trip cost itself changes.
    58	5. A site with `active = 0` cannot operate, deliver, or polish. Its inventory
    59	   may remain idle, but no operating variables can be positive.
    60	6. Tail risk is modeled as a CVaR surcharge at confidence level
    61	   `alpha = 0.8`, weighted by `tail_risk_weight = 0.35`.
    62
    63	## Parameters
    64
    65	From `sites.csv`:
    66
    67	- `M_s^max` = max electrolyzer modules
    68	- `cap_s` = kg/h per module
    69	- `rho_s^min` = minimum load fraction
    70	- `fmod_s` = daily fixed cost per selected electrolyzer module
    71	- `vprod_s` = variable production cost per kg
    72	- `B_s` = base storage kg
    73	- `K_s` = storage kg per tank module
    74	- `N_s^max` = max tank modules
    75	- `ftank_s` = daily fixed cost per selected tank module
    76	- `I_s^0` = initial inventory kg
    77	- `I_s^F` = minimum final inventory kg
    78	- `W_s` = daily water limit m3/day
    79	- `U_s` = startup limit over the day
    80	- `vpol_s` = polishing cost per kg
    81	- `fpol_s` = polishing fixed daily cost
    82
    83	From `customers.csv`:
    84
    85	- `floor_c` = minimum daily service fraction
    86	- `hcap_c` = maximum hourly shortfall fraction
    87	- `vshort_c` = shortfall penalty per kg
    88	- `prio_c` = priority weight
    89
    90	From `lanes.csv`:
    91
    92	- `orig_l` = origin site
    93	- `cust_l` = destination customer
    94	- `Q_l` = kg per trip
    95	- `Trips_l^base` = normal hourly trip cap
    96	- `dh_l` = driver-hours per trip
    97	- `ctrip_l` = cost per trip
    98
    99	From `scenarios.csv`:
   100
   101	- `p_w` = scenario probability
   102	- `multprice_w` = grid-price multiplier
   103	- `Ecap_w` = emissions cap
   104	- `Dcap_w` = hourly driver-hours cap
   105
   106	From `hourly_forecast.csv`:
   107
   108	- `R_{s,t}` = base renewable MWh
   109	- `gprice_{s,t}` = base grid price per MWh
   110	- `ge_{s,t}` = grid emissions kg per MWh
   111
   112	From `scenario_site_hourly.csv`:
   113
   114	- `rmult_{w,s,t}` = renewable multiplier
   115	- `cmult_{w,s,t}` = module capacity multiplier
   116	- `wmult_{w,s,t}` = water multiplier
   117	- `Gcap_{w,s,t}` = grid MWh cap
   118
   119	From `scenario_customer_hourly_demand.csv`:
   120
   121	- `dem_{w,c,t}` = realized hourly demand
   122
   123	From `scenario_lane_hourly.csv`:
   124
   125	- `Trips_{w,l,t}` = scenario hourly lane trip cap
   126	- `tmult_{w,l,t}` = driver-hours multiplier
   127	- `closed_{w,l,t}` = closure flag
   128
   129	Global constants from `config.json`:
   130
   131	- `e_prod` = electrolysis MWh per kg H2
   132	- `e_pol` = polishing MWh per kg
   133	- `w_prod` = water m3 per kg H2
   134	- `c_water` = water cost per m3
   135	- `c_start` = startup cost per module start
   136	- `c_emit` = emissions cost per kg
   137	- `c_vent` = vent penalty per kg
   138	- `Reserve` = minimum network reserve at the end of every hour
   139	- `alpha` = tail-risk confidence level
   140	- `lambda` = tail-risk weight
   141
   142	Useful derived constants:
   143
   144	- `StoreCap_s(y) = B_s + K_s * n_s`
   145	- `Cap_{w,s,t}(m) = cap_s * cmult_{w,s,t} * m`
   146	- `RenCap_{w,s,t} = R_{s,t} * rmult_{w,s,t}`
   147	- Effective lane closure bound:
   148	  `Trips_{w,l,t} = 0` whenever `closed_{w,l,t} = 1`
   149
   150	## Decision variables
   151
   152	### First stage
   153
   154	For each site `s`:
   155
   156	- `a_s in {0,1}`: site active flag
   157	- `m_s in Z_+`: selected electrolyzer modules
   158	- `n_s in Z_+`: selected tank modules
   159	- `p_s in {0,1}`: polishing active flag
   160
   161	### Second stage, for each scenario `w`, hour `t`, site `s`
   162
   163	- `on_{w,s,t} in Z_+`: online modules
   164	- `start_{w,s,t} in Z_+`: module starts
   165	- `prod_{w,s,t} >= 0`: produced hydrogen kg
   166	- `ren_{w,s,t} >= 0`: renewable MWh used
   167	- `grid_{w,s,t} >= 0`: grid MWh used
   168	- `pol_{w,s,t} >= 0`: hydrogen polished kg
   169	- `uinv_{w,s,t} >= 0`: unpolished inventory at end of hour
   170	- `pinv_{w,s,t} >= 0`: polished inventory at end of hour
   171	- `vent_u_{w,s,t} >= 0`, `vent_p_{w,s,t} >= 0`: vented kg by purity class
   172
   173	For each scenario `w`, hour `t`, lane `l`
   174
   175	- `trip_{w,l,t} in Z_+`: trips
   176	- `del_{w,l,t} >= 0`: delivered kg
   177
   178	For each scenario `w`, hour `t`, customer `c`
   179
   180	- `short_{w,c,t} >= 0`: shortfall kg
   181
   182	Tail-risk variables:
   183
   184	- `eta` free: VaR anchor
   185	- `xi_w >= 0`: positive excess of scenario operating cost above `eta`
   186
   187	## Objective
   188
   189	Minimize
   190
   191	`InfraCost + ExpectedOpCost + lambda * CVaRalpha(OpCost_w)`
   192
   193	where
   194
   195	- `InfraCost =
   196	  sum_s (fmod_s * m_s + ftank_s * n_s + fpol_s * p_s)`
   197
   198	- `OpCost_w =
   199	  sum_{s,t} [vprod_s * prod_{w,s,t}
   200	           + c_water * w_prod * prod_{w,s,t}
   201	           + c_start * start_{w,s,t}
   202	           + vpol_s * pol_{w,s,t}
   203	           + c_vent * (vent_u_{w,s,t} + vent_p_{w,s,t})
   204	           + multprice_w * gprice_{s,t} * grid_{w,s,t}
   205	           + c_emit * ge_{s,t} * grid_{w,s,t}]
   206	  + sum_{l,t} ctrip_l * trip_{w,l,t}
   207	  + sum_{c,t} (vshort_c * prio_c) * short_{w,c,t}`
   208
   209	- `ExpectedOpCost = sum_w p_w * OpCost_w`
   210
   211	- `CVaRalpha(OpCost_w) = eta + (1 / (1 - alpha)) * sum_w p_w * xi_w`
   212
   213	with
   214
   215	- `xi_w >= OpCost_w - eta`
   216	- `xi_w >= 0`
   217
   218	## Constraints
   219
   220	### 1. Infrastructure selection
   221
   222	For all sites `s`:
   223
   224	- `0 <= m_s <= M_s^max * a_s`
   225	- `0 <= n_s <= N_s^max * a_s`
   226	- `0 <= p_s <= a_s`
   227
   228	These are the nonanticipative first-stage decisions and are shared by all
   229	scenarios.
   230
   231	### 2. Online modules and starts
   232
   233	For all `w,s,t`:
   234
   235	- `0 <= on_{w,s,t} <= m_s`
   236	- `start_{w,s,0} >= on_{w,s,0}`
   237	- `start_{w,s,t} >= on_{w,s,t} - on_{w,s,t-1}` for `t >= 1`
   238	- `sum_t start_{w,s,t} <= U_s`
   239
   240	Because starts have positive cost and a hard daily cap, the model will set them
   241	to the true positive changes in online count.
   242
   243	### 3. Production bounds
   244
   245	For all `w,s,t`:
   246
   247	- `prod_{w,s,t} <= cap_s * cmult_{w,s,t} * on_{w,s,t}`
   248	- `prod_{w,s,t} >= rho_s^min * cap_s * cmult_{w,s,t} * on_{w,s,t}`
   249
   250	This enforces the minimum stable load whenever online modules are positive.
   251
   252	### 4. Energy balance and limits
   253
   254	For all `w,s,t`:
   255
   256	- `e_prod * prod_{w,s,t} + e_pol * pol_{w,s,t} <= ren_{w,s,t} + grid_{w,s,t}`
   257	- `0 <= ren_{w,s,t} <= RenCap_{w,s,t}`
   258	- `0 <= grid_{w,s,t} <= Gcap_{w,s,t}`
   259
   260	### 5. Water permits

   640	        if value < best:

   646	def validate_solution(data, solution):
   647	    tol = data["tol"]
   648	    hours = data["hours"]
   649	    site_ids = data["site_ids"]
   650	    customer_ids = data["customer_ids"]
   651	    scenario_ids = data["scenario_ids"]
   652	    sites = data["sites"]
   653	    customers = data["customers"]
   654	    lanes = data["lanes"]
   655	    scenarios = data["scenarios"]
   656	    forecast = data["forecast"]
   657	    scenario_site = data["scenario_site"]
   658	    scenario_demand = data["scenario_demand"]
   659	    scenario_lane = data["scenario_lane"]
   660	    config = data["config"]
   661
   662	    issues = []
   663	    group_residuals = defaultdict(float)
   664	    integrality_residual = 0.0
   665
   666	    def record(group, residual, scale, message):
   667	        normalized = abs(residual) / max(1.0, scale)
   668	        group_residuals[group] = max(group_residuals[group], normalized)
   669	        if normalized > tol:
   670	            issues.append(f"{group}: {message} (residual={residual:.8g}, normalized={normalized:.8g})")
   671
   672	    def parse_bool(value):
   673	        if isinstance(value, bool):
   674	            return value
   675	        if value in (0, 1):
   676	            return bool(value)
   677	        raise ValueError(f"Expected boolean, got {value!r}")
   678
   679	    if set(solution.keys()) != {"infrastructure", "operations", "deliveries", "shortfalls"}:
   680	        issues.append(f"top-level keys mismatch: {sorted(solution.keys())}")
   681
   682	    infra = {}
   683	    if len(solution.get("infrastructure", [])) != len(site_ids):
   684	        issues.append("infrastructure row count mismatch")
   685	    for row in solution.get("infrastructure", []):
   686	        site_id = row.get("site_id")
   687	        if site_id not in sites or site_id in infra:
   688	            issues.append(f"invalid infrastructure row {site_id}")
   689	            continue
   690	        modules = row.get("electrolyzer_modules")
   691	        tanks = row.get("tank_modules")
   692	        if abs(round(modules) - modules) > 1e-8:
   693	            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(modules) - modules))
   694	        if abs(round(tanks) - tanks) > 1e-8:
   695	            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(tanks) - tanks))
   696	        active = parse_bool(row.get("active"))
   697	        polish = parse_bool(row.get("polishing_active"))
   698	        modules = int(round(modules))
   699	        tanks = int(round(tanks))
   700	        infra[site_id] = {
   701	            "active": active,
   702	            "modules": modules,
   703	            "tanks": tanks,
   704	            "polishing_active": polish,
   705	        }
   706	        record("infra_modules", max(0.0, modules - sites[site_id]["max_modules"]), sites[site_id]["max_modules"], site_id)
   707	        record("infra_tanks", max(0.0, tanks - sites[site_id]["max_tank_modules"]), sites[site_id]["max_tank_modules"], site_id)
   708	        if not active:
   709	            record("infra_active", modules + tanks + (1 if polish else 0), 1.0, site_id)
   710
   711	    for site_id in site_ids:
   712	        infra.setdefault(site_id, {"active": False, "modules": 0, "tanks": 0, "polishing_active": False})
   713
   714	    ops = {}
   715	    expected_ops = len(scenario_ids) * len(hours) * len(site_ids)
   716	    if len(solution.get("operations", [])) != expected_ops:
   717	        issues.append("operations row count mismatch")
   718	    for row in solution.get("operations", []):
   719	        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("site_id"))
   720	        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in sites or key in ops:
   721	            issues.append(f"invalid operations row {key}")
   722	            continue
   723	        online = row.get("online_modules")
   724	        startup = row.get("startup_modules")
   725	        if abs(round(online) - online) > 1e-8:
   726	            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(online) - online))
   727	        if abs(round(startup) - startup) > 1e-8:
   728	            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(startup) - startup))
   729	        ops[key] = {
   730	            "online": int(round(online)),
   731	            "startup": int(round(startup)),
   732	            "production": float(row.get("production_kg")),
   733	            "renewable": float(row.get("renewable_mwh")),
   734	            "grid": float(row.get("grid_mwh")),
   735	            "storage": float(row.get("storage_end_kg")),
   736	            "vent": float(row.get("vent_kg")),
   737	            "water": float(row.get("water_m3")),
   738	            "polishing": float(row.get("polishing_kg")),
   739	        }
   740	    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
   741	        for hour in hours:
   742	            for site_id in site_ids:
   743	                ops.setdefault(
   744	                    (scenario_id, hour, site_id),
   745	                    {"online": 0, "startup": 0, "production": 0.0, "renewable": 0.0, "grid": 0.0, "storage": 0.0, "vent": 0.0, "water": 0.0, "polishing": 0.0},
   746	                )
   747
   748	    deliveries = {}
   749	    inbound = defaultdict(float)
   750	    outbound = defaultdict(float)
   751	    outbound_low = defaultdict(float)
   752	    outbound_high = defaultdict(float)
   753	    driver_hours = defaultdict(float)
   754	    for row in solution.get("deliveries", []):
   755	        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("lane_id"))
   756	        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in lanes or key in deliveries:
   757	            issues.append(f"invalid delivery row {key}")
   758	            continue
   759	        trips = row.get("trips")
   760	        if abs(round(trips) - trips) > 1e-8:
   761	            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(trips) - trips))
   762	        trips = int(round(trips))
   763	        kg = float(row.get("kg"))
   764	        deliveries[key] = {"trips": trips, "kg": kg}
   765	        lane = lanes[key[2]]
   766	        inbound[(key[0], key[1], lane["customer_id"])] += kg
   767	        outbound[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
   768	        if lane["customer_id"] in data["high_purity_customers"]:
   769	            outbound_high[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
   770	        else:
   771	            outbound_low[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
   772	        sl = scenario_lane[(key[0], key[2], key[1])]
   773	        driver_hours[(key[0], key[1])] += trips * lane["driver_hours_per_trip"] * sl["travel_time_multiplier"]
   774	        record("trip_cap", max(0.0, trips - sl["max_trips"]), max(1.0, sl["max_trips"]), str(key))
   775	        record("payload", max(0.0, kg - lane["kg_per_trip"] * trips), max(1.0, lane["kg_per_trip"]), str(key))
   776	        if not infra[lane["origin_site"]]["active"]:
   777	            record("inactive_delivery", trips + kg, 1.0, str(key))
   778
   779	    shorts = {}
   780	    expected_shorts = len(scenario_ids) * len(hours) * len(customer_ids)
   781	    if len(solution.get("shortfalls", [])) != expected_shorts:
   782	        issues.append("shortfalls row count mismatch")
   783	    for row in solution.get("shortfalls", []):
   784	        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("customer_id"))
   785	        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in customers or key in shorts:
   786	            issues.append(f"invalid shortfall row {key}")
   787	            continue
   788	        shorts[key] = float(row.get("kg"))
   789	    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
   790	        for hour in hours:
   791	            for customer_id in customer_ids:
   792	                shorts.setdefault((scenario_id, hour, customer_id), 0.0)
   793
   794	    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
   795	        scenario = scenarios[scenario_id]
   796	        emissions = 0.0
   797	        for hour in hours:
   798	            record(
   799	                "driver_hours",
   800	                max(0.0, driver_hours[(scenario_id, hour)] - scenario["driver_hours_cap"]),
   801	                max(1.0, scenario["driver_hours_cap"]),
   802	                f"{scenario_id},{hour}",
   803	            )
   804	            reserve = sum(ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]["storage"] for site_id in site_ids)
   805	            record(
   806	                "reserve",
   807	                max(0.0, config["system_min_reserve_kg"] - reserve),
   808	                max(1.0, config["system_min_reserve_kg"]),
   809	                f"{scenario_id},{hour}",
   810	            )
   811
   812	        for customer_id in customer_ids:
   813	            customer = customers[customer_id]
   814	            total_short = 0.0
   815	            for hour in hours:
   816	                demand = scenario_demand[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
   817	                delivered = inbound[(scenario_id, hour, customer_id)]
   818	                short = shorts[(scenario_id, hour, customer_id)]
   819	                total_short += short
   820	                record("demand_balance", delivered + short - demand, max(1.0, demand), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{customer_id}")
   821	                record(
   822	                    "hourly_shortfall",
   823	                    max(0.0, short - customer["max_hourly_shortfall_fraction"] * demand),
   824	                    max(1.0, demand),
   825	                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{customer_id}",
   826	                )
   827	            record(
   828	                "service_floor",
   829	                max(0.0, total_short - (1.0 - customer["service_floor_fraction"]) * data["daily_demand"][(scenario_id, customer_id)]),
   830	                max(1.0, data["daily_demand"][(scenario_id, customer_id)]),
   831	                f"{scenario_id},{customer_id}",
   832	            )
   833
   834	        for site_id in site_ids:
   835	            site = sites[site_id]
   836	            storage_cap = site["tank_base_kg"] + site["tank_module_capacity_kg"] * infra[site_id]["tanks"]
   837	            water_total = 0.0
   838	            startup_total = 0.0
   839	            p_lo = 0.0
   840	            p_hi = 0.0
   841	            for hour in hours:
   842	                row = ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]
   843	                sh = scenario_site[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
   844	                fc = forecast[(site_id, hour)]
   845	                cap = site["module_capacity_kgph"] * sh["module_capacity_multiplier"]
   846	                prev_storage = site["initial_inventory_kg"] if hour == 0 else ops[(scenario_id, hour - 1, site_id)]["storage"]
   847	                prev_online = 0 if hour == 0 else ops[(scenario_id, hour - 1, site_id)]["online"]
   848
   849	                record("online", max(0.0, row["online"] - infra[site_id]["modules"]), max(1.0, infra[site_id]["modules"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   850	                if not infra[site_id]["active"]:
   851	                    record(
   852	                        "inactive_ops",
   853	                        row["online"] + row["startup"] + row["production"] + row["renewable"] + row["grid"] + row["vent"] + row["polishing"],
   854	                        1.0,
   855	                        f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
   856	                    )
   857	                record("prod_upper", max(0.0, row["production"] - cap * row["online"]), max(1.0, cap), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   858	                record(
   859	                    "prod_lower",
   860	                    max(0.0, site["min_load_fraction"] * cap * row["online"] - row["production"]),
   861	                    max(1.0, cap),
   862	                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
   863	                )

   865	                    "energy",

   867	                    max(1.0, row["renewable"] + row["grid"]),
   868	                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
   869	                )
   870	                record("renewable_cap", max(0.0, row["renewable"] - fc["renewable_mwh"] * sh["renewable_multiplier"]), max(1.0, fc["renewable_mwh"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   871	                record("grid_cap", max(0.0, row["grid"] - sh["grid_cap_mwh"]), max(1.0, sh["grid_cap_mwh"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   872	                record("water_link", row["water"] - config["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * row["production"], max(1.0, row["water"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   873	                record(
   874	                    "polishing_active",
   875	                    max(0.0, row["polishing"] - data["max_polish"][site_id] * (1 if infra[site_id]["polishing_active"] else 0)),
   876	                    max(1.0, data["max_polish"][site_id]),
   877	                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
   878	                )
   879	                record("storage_cap", max(0.0, row["storage"] - storage_cap), max(1.0, storage_cap), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   880	                record(
   881	                    "inventory_balance",
   882	                    row["storage"] - prev_storage - row["production"] + outbound[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] + row["vent"],
   883	                    max(1.0, prev_storage + row["production"]),
   884	                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
   885	                )
   886	                record("startup_delta", max(0.0, row["online"] - prev_online - row["startup"]), max(1.0, row["online"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
   887
   888	                p_lo = max(0.0, p_lo + row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] - row["vent"])
   889	                p_hi = min(row["storage"], p_hi + row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)])
   890	                if hour == 0:
   891	                    p_lo = max(0.0, row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] - row["vent"])
   892	                    p_hi = min(row["storage"], row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)])
   893	                if p_lo > p_hi + tol:
   894	                    issues.append(f"purity_balance: {scenario_id},{hour},{site_id} has no feasible polished inventory split")
   895	                    group_residuals["purity_balance"] = max(group_residuals["purity_balance"], p_lo - p_hi)
   896
   897	                water_total += row["water"]
   898	                startup_total += row["startup"]
   899	                emissions += fc["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"] * row["grid"]
   900
   901	            record("startup_limit", max(0.0, startup_total - site["startup_limit"]), max(1.0, site["startup_limit"]), f"{scenario_id},{site_id}")
   902	            record(
   903	                "water_limit",
   904	                max(0.0, water_total - site["water_limit_m3_per_day"] * data["water_multiplier"][(scenario_id, site_id)]),
   905	                max(1.0, site["water_limit_m3_per_day"]),
   906	                f"{scenario_id},{site_id}",
   907	            )
   908	            record(
   909	                "final_inventory",
   910	                max(0.0, site["final_min_inventory_kg"] - ops[(scenario_id, hours[-1], site_id)]["storage"]),
   911	                max(1.0, site["final_min_inventory_kg"]),
   912	                f"{scenario_id},{site_id}",
   913	            )
   914
   915	        record("emission_cap", max(0.0, emissions - scenario["emission_cap_kg"]), max(1.0, scenario["emission_cap_kg"]), scenario_id)
   916
   917	    infra_cost = sum(
   918	        sites[site_id]["module_daily_cost"] * infra[site_id]["modules"]
   919	        + sites[site_id]["tank_daily_cost"] * infra[site_id]["tanks"]
   920	        + sites[site_id]["polishing_daily_cost"] * (1 if infra[site_id]["polishing_active"] else 0)
   921	        for site_id in site_ids
   922	    )
   923	    op_costs = {}
   924	    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
   925	        total = 0.0
   926	        for site_id in site_ids:
   927	            for hour in hours:
   928	                row = ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]
   929	                fc = forecast[(site_id, hour)]
   930	                total += sites[site_id]["variable_cost_per_kg"] * row["production"]

   932	                total += config["startup_cost_per_module"] * row["startup"]
   933	                total += sites[site_id]["polishing_cost_per_kg"] * row["polishing"]
   934	                total += config["vent_penalty_per_kg"] * row["vent"]
   935	                total += scenarios[scena

   940	        for (scen, _, customer_id), short in shorts.items():
   941	            if scen == scenario_id:
   942	                total += customers[customer_id]["shortfall_cost_per_kg"] * customers[customer_id]["priority_weight"] * short
   943	        op_costs[scenario_id] = total
   944
   945	    expected_cost = sum(scenarios[w]["probability"] * op_costs[w] for w in scenario_ids)
   946	    cvar_value, cvar_eta = compute_cvar([(scenarios[w]["probability"], op_costs[w]) for w in scenario_ids], config["tail_risk_alpha"])
   947	    objective = infra_cost + expected_cost + config["tail_risk_weight"] * cvar_value
   948
   949	    return {
   950	        "valid": not issues,
   951	        "issues": issues,
   952	        "max_normalized_residual": max(group_residuals.values(), default=0.0),
   953	        "violated_constraint_groups": sum(1 for value in group_residuals.values() if value > tol),
   954	        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
   955	        "group_residuals": dict(sorted(group_residuals.items())),

   958	            "operations_rows": len(solution.get("operations", [])),
   959	            "shortfall_rows": len(solution.get("shortfalls", [])),
   960	            "delivery_rows": len(solution.get("deliveries", [])),
   961	        },
   962	        "infrastructure_cost": infra_cost,
   963	        "scenario_operating_costs": op_costs,
   964	        "expected_operating_cost": expected_cost,
   965	        "cvar_value": cvar_value,
   966	        "cvar_eta": cvar_eta,
   967	        "objective": objective,
   968	    }

   970
   971	def run_negative_audit(data, solution):
   972	    corrupted = copy.deepcopy(solution)
   973	    if corrupted["shortfalls"]:
   974	        corrupted["shortfalls"][0]["kg"] = clean_float(corrupted["shortfalls"][0]["kg"] + 1.0)
   975	    elif corrupted["operations"]:
   976	        corrupted["operations"][0]["storage_end_kg"] = clean_float(corrupted["operations"][0]["storage_end_kg"] + 1.0)
   977	    audit = validate_solution(data, corrupted)
   978	    return not audit["valid"]

   980
   981	def write_solution(solution):
   982	    SOLUTION_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(solution, indent=2) + "\n")

   984
   985	def safe_gap(model):
   986	    try:
   987	        return model.getGap()
   988	    except Exception:
   989	        return None
   990
   991
   992	def safe_obj(model):
   993	    try:
   994	        return model.getObjVal()
   995	    except Exception:
   996	        return None
   997
   998
   999	def safe_bound(model):
  1000	    try:
  1001	        return model.getDualbound()
  1002	    except Exception:
  1003	        return None

  1006	def write_log(command, time_limit, elapsed, model, validation, negative_audit_ok):
  1007	    status = str(model.getStatus())
  1008	    nsols = model.getNSols()
  1009	    obj = safe_obj(model)

  1027	        lines.append(f"- Solver objective: `{obj:.8f}`")
  1028	    if bound is not None:
  1029	        lines.append(f"- Best bound: `{bound:.8f}`")

  1042	            f"- Integrality residual: `{validation['integrality_residual']:.8g}`",
  1043	            f"- Negative audit rejected corrupted copy: `{negative_audit_ok}`",
  1044	            f"- Coverage: `{validation['coverage']}`",
  1045	            f"- Infrastructure cost: `{validation['infrastructure_cost']:.8f}`",
  1046	            f"- Expected operating cost: `{validation['expected_operating_cost']:.8f}`",

  1062	        for issue in validation["issues"][:50]:
  1063	            lines.append(f"- {issue}")
  1064	    else:
  1065	        lines.append("- No validation issues detected.")
  1066
  1067	    LOG_PATH.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

  1070	def main():
  1071	    start = time.time()
  1072	    time_limit = parse_time_limit()
  1073	    data = build_data()
  1074	    model, vars_dict = build_model(data, time_limit)
  1075	    model.optimize()
  1076
  1077	    if model.getNSols() <= 0:
  1078	        raise RuntimeError(f"SCIP ended with status {model.getStatus()} and no incumbent solution")
  1079
  1080	    solution = extract_solution(data, model, vars_dict)
  1081	    write_solution(solution)
  1082	    validation = validate_solution(data, solution)
  1083	    if not validation["valid"]:
  1084	        raise RuntimeError("Exported solution failed independent validation")
  1085
  1086	    negative_audit_ok = run_negative_audit(data, solution)
  1087	    elapsed = time.time() - start
  1088	    write_log(sys.argv, time_limit, elapsed, model, validation, negative_audit_ok)

  1091	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1092	    main()

    tank_modules = {}
    polishing_active = {}
    for site_id in site_ids:
        site = sites[site_id]
        active[site_id] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"active[{site_id}]")
        modules[site_id] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0, ub=site["max_modules"], name=f"modules[{site_id}]")
        tank_modules[site_id] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0, ub=site["max_tank_modules"], name=f"tank[{site_id}]")
        polishing_active[site_id] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"polishing[{site_id}]")

    online = {}
    startup = {}
    production = {}
    renewable = {}
    grid = {}
    polished = {}
    unpolished_inventory = {}
    polished_inventory = {}
    vent_unpolished = {}
    vent_polished = {}
    trips = {}
    delivered = {}
    shortfall = {}
    excess = {}

    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for site_id in site_ids:
            site = sites[site_id]
            for hour in hours:
                key = (scenario_id, site_id, hour)
                online[key] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0, ub=site["max_modules"], name=f"on[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                startup[key] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0, ub=site["max_modules"], name=f"start[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                production[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"prod[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                renewable[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"ren[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                grid[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"grid[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                polished[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"pol[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                unpolished_inventory[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"uinv[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                polished_inventory[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"pinv[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                vent_unpolished[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"ventu[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                vent_polished[key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"ventp[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
        for lane_id in lane_ids:
            lane = lanes[lane_id]
            for hour in hours:
                lane_key = (scenario_id, lane_id, hour)
                cap = scenario_lane[lane_key]["max_trips"]
                trips[lane_key] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0, ub=cap, name=f"trip[{scenario_id},{lane_id},{hour}]")
                delivered[lane_key] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=lane["kg_per_trip"] * cap, name=f"del[{scenario_id},{lane_id},{hour}]")
        for customer_id in customer_ids:
            for hour in hours:
                shortfall[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"short[{scenario_id},{customer_id},{hour}]")
        excess[scenario_id] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"excess[{scenario_id}]")

    eta = model.addVar(lb=-model.infinity(), name="eta")

    for site_id in site_ids:
        site = sites[site_id]
        model.addCons(modules[site_id] <= site["max_modules"] * active[site_id], name=f"module_select[{site_id}]")
        model.addCons(
            tank_modules[site_id] <= site["max_tank_modules"] * active[site_id],
            name=f"tank_select[{site_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(polishing_active[site_id] <= active[site_id], name=f"polish_select[{site_id}]")

    operating_cost_expr = {}
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        scenario = scenarios[scenario_id]
        for site_id in site_ids:
            site = sites[site_id]
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(startup[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)] for hour in hours) <= site["startup_limit"],
                name=f"startup_limit[{scenario_id},{site_id}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(config["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * production[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)] for hour in hours)
                <= site["water_limit_m3_per_day"] * data["water_multiplier"][(scenario_id, site_id)],
                name=f"water[{scenario_id},{site_id}]",
            )
            for hour in hours:
                key = (scenario_id, site_id, hour)
                sh = scenario_site[key]
                fc = forecast[(site_id, hour)]
                low_delivery = quicksum(
                    delivered[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)] for lane_id in data["lanes_by_site_low"].get(site_id, [])
                )
                high_delivery = quicksum(
                    delivered[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)] for lane_id in data["lanes_by_site_high"].get(site_id, [])
                )

                model.addCons(online[key] <= modules[site_id], name=f"online[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                if hour == 0:
                    model.addCons(startup[key] >= online[key], name=f"startup0[{scenario_id},{site_id}]")
                else:
                    prev = (scenario_id, site_id, hour - 1)
                    model.addCons(startup[key] >= online[key] - online[prev], name=f"startup[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")

                cap = site["module_capacity_kgph"] * sh["module_capacity_multiplier"]
                model.addCons(production[key] <= cap * online[key], name=f"produb[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                model.addCons(
                    production[key] >= site["min_load_fraction"] * cap * online[key],
                    name=f"prodlb[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                )
                model.addCons(
                    config["energy_mwh_per_kg_h2"] * production[key]
                    + config["polishing_mwh_per_kg"] * polished[key]
                    <= renewable[key] + grid[key],
                    name=f"energy[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                )
                model.addCons(
                    renewable[key] <= fc["renewable_mwh"] * sh["renewable_multiplier"],
                    name=f"rencap[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                )
                model.addCons(grid[key] <= sh["grid_cap_mwh"], name=f"gridcap[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]")
                model.addCons(
                    polished[key] <= data["max_polish"][site_id] * polishing_active[site_id],
                    name=f"polcap[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                )

                if hour == 0:
                    model.addCons(
                        unpolished_inventory[key]
                        == site["initial_inventory_kg"] + production[key] - polished[key] - low_delivery - vent_unpolished[key],
                        name=f"uinv0[{scenario_id},{site_id}]",
                    )
                    model.addCons(
                        polished_inventory[key] == polished[key] - high_delivery - vent_polished[key],
                        name=f"pinv0[{scenario_id},{site_id}]",
                    )
                else:
                    prev = (scenario_id, site_id, hour - 1)
                    model.addCons(
                        unpolished_inventory[key]
                        == unpolished_inventory[prev] + production[key] - polished[key] - low_delivery - vent_unpolished[key],
                        name=f"uinv[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                    )
                    model.addCons(
                        polished_inventory[key]
                        == polished_inventory[prev] + polished[key] - high_delivery - vent_polished[key],
                        name=f"pinv[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                    )

                model.addCons(
                    unpolished_inventory[key] + polished_inventory[key]
                    <= site["tank_base_kg"] + site["tank_module_capacity_kg"] * tank_modules[site_id],
                    name=f"storage[{scenario_id},{site_id},{hour}]",
                )

            final_key = (scenario_id, site_id, hours[-1])
            model.addCons(
                unpolished_inventory[final_key] + polished_inventory[final_key] >= site["final_min_inventory_kg"],
                name=f"final[{scenario_id},{site_id}]",
            )

        for lane_id in lane_ids:
            lane = lanes[lane_id]
            origin = lane["origin_site"]
            for hour in hours:
                lane_key = (scenario_id, lane_id, hour)
                model.addCons(
                    trips[lane_key] <= scenario_lane[lane_key]["max_trips"] * active[origin],
                    name=f"tripactive[{scenario_id},{lane_id},{hour}]",
                )
                model.addCons(
                    delivered[lane_key] <= lane["kg_per_trip"] * trips[lane_key],
                    name=f"payload[{scenario_id},{lane_id},{hour}]",
                )

        for hour in hours:
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    lanes[lane_id]["driver_hours_per_trip"]
                    * scenario_lane[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)]["travel_time_multiplier"]
                    * trips[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)]
                    for lane_id in lane_ids
                )
                <= scenario["driver_hours_cap"],
                name=f"drivers[{scenario_id},{hour}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    unpolished_inventory[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)] + polished_inventory[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                    for site_id in site_ids
                )
                >= config["system_min_reserve_kg"],
                name=f"reserve[{scenario_id},{hour}]",
            )

        for customer_id in customer_ids:
            customer = customers[customer_id]
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(shortfall[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)] for hour in hours)
                <= (1.0 - customer["service_floor_fraction"]) * data["daily_demand"][(scenario_id, customer_id)],
                name=f"service[{scenario_id},{customer_id}]",
            )
            for hour in hours:
                demand = scenario_demand[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
                model.addCons(
                    quicksum(delivered[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)] for lane_id in data["lanes_by_customer"][customer_id])
                    + shortfall[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
                    == demand,
                    name=f"demand[{scenario_id},{customer_id},{hour}]",
                )
                model.addCons(
                    shortfall[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
                    <= customer["max_hourly_shortfall_fraction"] * demand,
                    name=f"shortcap[{scenario_id},{customer_id},{hour}]",
                )

        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                forecast[(site_id, hour)]["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"] * grid[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                for site_id in site_ids
                for hour in hours
            )
            <= scenario["emission_cap_kg"],
            name=f"emissions[{scenario_id}]",
        )

        operating_cost_expr[scenario_id] = (
            quicksum(
                sites[site_id]["variable_cost_per_kg"] * production[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                + config["water_cost_per_m3"] * config["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * production[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                + config["startup_cost_per_module"] * startup[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                + sites[site_id]["polishing_cost_per_kg"] * polished[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                + config["vent_penalty_per_kg"]
                * (vent_unpolished[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)] + vent_polished[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)])
                + scenario["grid_price_multiplier"]
                * forecast[(site_id, hour)]["grid_price_per_mwh"]
                * grid[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                + config["emission_cost_per_kg"]
                * forecast[(site_id, hour)]["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"]
                * grid[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                for site_id in site_ids
                for hour in hours
            )
            + quicksum(
                lanes[lane_id]["cost_per_trip"] * trips[(scenario_id, lane_id, hour)]
                for lane_id in lane_ids
                for hour in hours
            )
            + quicksum(
                customers[customer_id]["shortfall_cost_per_kg"]
                * customers[customer_id]["priority_weight"]
                * shortfall[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
                for customer_id in customer_ids
                for hour in hours
            )
        )
        model.addCons(excess[scenario_id] >= operating_cost_expr[scenario_id] - eta, name=f"cvar[{scenario_id}]")

    infra_cost = quicksum(
        sites[site_id]["module_daily_cost"] * modules[site_id]
        + sites[site_id]["tank_daily_cost"] * tank_modules[site_id]
        + sites[site_id]["polishing_daily_cost"] * polishing_active[site_id]
        for site_id in site_ids
    )
    expected_cost = quicksum(
        scenarios[scenario_id]["probability"] * operating_cost_expr[scenario_id] for scenario_id in scenario_ids
    )
    cvar_cost = eta + (1.0 / (1.0 - config["tail_risk_alpha"])) * quicksum(
        scenarios[scenario_id]["probability"] * excess[scenario_id] for scenario_id in scenario_ids
    )

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import copy
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import sys
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum


ROOT = Path("/app")
DATA_DIR = ROOT / "data"
SUBMISSIONS_DIR = ROOT / "submissions"
SOLUTION_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solution.json"
LOG_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solve_log.md"
GAP_LIMIT = 0.0005
DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT = 300.0
HIGH_PURITY_THRESHOLD = 99.9


def load_json(path):
    with path.open() as handle:
        return json.load(handle)


def load_csv(path):
    with path.open(newline="") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def as_float(value):
    return float(value)


def as_int(value):
    return int(round(float(value)))


def clean_float(value, digits=8):
    if abs(value) < 1e-10:
        value = 0.0
    return float(f"{value:.{digits}f}")


def clean_int(value):
    return int(round(value))


def parse_time_limit():
    raw = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if not raw:
        return DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT
    try:
        return max(1.0, min(DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT, float(raw)))
    except ValueError:
        return DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT


def build_data():
    config = load_json(DATA_DIR / "config.json")
    tol = as_float(config["numerical_tolerance"])
    hours = list(range(as_int(config["planning_horizon_hours"])))

    sites = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "sites.csv"):
        site_id = row["site_id"]
        sites[site_id] = {
            "site_id": site_id,
            "region": row["region"],
            "max_modules": as_int(row["max_modules"]),
            "module_capacity_kgph": as_float(row["module_capacity_kgph"]),
            "min_load_fraction": as_float(row["min_load_fraction"]),
            "module_daily_cost": as_float(row["module_daily_cost"]),
            "variable_cost_per_kg": as_float(row["variable_cost_per_kg"]),
            "tank_base_kg": as_float(row["tank_base_kg"]),
            "tank_module_capacity_kg": as_float(row["tank_module_capacity_kg"]),
            "max_tank_modules": as_int(row["max_tank_modules"]),
            "tank_daily_cost": as_float(row["tank_daily_cost"]),
            "initial_inventory_kg": as_float(row["initial_inventory_kg"]),
            "final_min_inventory_kg": as_float(row["final_min_inventory_kg"]),
            "water_limit_m3_per_day": as_float(row["water_limit_m3_per_day"]),
            "startup_limit": as_int(row["startup_limit"]),
            "polishing_cost_per_kg": as_float(row["polishing_cost_per_kg"]),
            "polishing_daily_cost": as_float(row["polishing_daily_cost"]),
        }

    customers = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "customers.csv"):
        customer_id = row["customer_id"]
        customers[customer_id] = {
            "customer_id": customer_id,
            "segment": row["segment"],
            "region": row["region"],
            "purity_required_pct": as_float(row["purity_required_pct"]),
            "service_floor_fraction": as_float(row["service_floor_fraction"]),
            "max_hourly_shortfall_fraction": as_float(row["max_hourly_shortfall_fraction"]),
            "shortfall_cost_per_kg": as_float(row["shortfall_cost_per_kg"]),
            "priority_weight": as_float(row["priority_weight"]),
        }

    lanes = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "lanes.csv"):
        lane_id = row["lane_id"]
        lanes[lane_id] = {
            "lane_id": lane_id,
            "origin_site": row["origin_site"],
            "customer_id": row["customer_id"],
            "distance_km": as_float(row["distance_km"]),
            "kg_per_trip": as_float(row["kg_per_trip"]),
            "base_max_trips_per_hour": as_int(row["base_max_trips_per_hour"]),
            "driver_hours_per_trip": as_float(row["driver_hours_per_trip"]),
            "cost_per_trip": as_float(row["cost_per_trip"]),
            "hazmat_window_start": as_int(row["hazmat_window_start"]),
            "hazmat_window_end": as_int(row["hazmat_window_end"]),
        }

    scenarios = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenarios.csv"):
        scenario_id = row["scenario_id"]
        scenarios[scenario_id] = {
            "scenario_id": scenario_id,
            "probability": as_float(row["probability"]),
            "description": row["description"],
            "grid_price_multiplier": as_float(row["grid_price_multiplier"]),
            "emission_cap_kg": as_float(row["emission_cap_kg"]),
            "driver_hours_cap": as_float(row["driver_hours_cap"]),
        }

    forecast = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "hourly_forecast.csv"):
        forecast[(row["site_id"], as_int(row["hour"]))] = {
            "renewable_mwh": as_float(row["renewable_mwh"]),
            "grid_price_per_mwh": as_float(row["grid_price_per_mwh"]),
            "grid_emission_kg_per_mwh": as_float(row["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"]),
        }

    scenario_site = {}
    water_multiplier = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenario_site_hourly.csv"):
        key = (row["scenario_id"], row["site_id"], as_int(row["hour"]))
        scenario_site[key] = {
            "renewable_multiplier": as_float(row["renewable_multiplier"]),
            "module_capacity_multiplier": as_float(row["module_capacity_multiplier"]),
            "water_limit_multiplier": as_float(row["water_limit_multiplier"]),
            "grid_cap_mwh": as_float(row["grid_cap_mwh"]),
        }
        ws_key = (row["scenario_id"], row["site_id"])
        value = scenario_site[key]["water_limit_multiplier"]
        prior = water_multiplier.get(ws_key)
        if prior is None:
            water_multiplier[ws_key] = value
        elif abs(prior - value) > tol:
            raise ValueError(f"Water multiplier varies within scenario-site {ws_key}")

    scenario_demand = {}
    daily_demand = defaultdict(float)
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenario_customer_hourly_demand.csv"):
        key = (row["scenario_id"], row["customer_id"], as_int(row["hour"]))
        demand = as_float(row["demand_kg"])
        scenario_demand[key] = demand
        daily_demand[(row["scenario_id"], row["customer_id"])] += demand

    scenario_lane = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenario_lane_hourly.csv"):
        scenario_lane[(row["scenario_id"], row["lane_id"], as_int(row["hour"]))] = {
            "max_trips": as_int(row["max_trips"]),
            "travel_time_multiplier": as_float(row["travel_time_multiplier"]),
            "closed": as_int(row["closed"]),
        }

    base_demand = {
        (row["customer_id"], as_int(row["hour"])): as_float(row["base_demand_kg"])
        for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "base_customer_hourly_demand.csv")
    }

    site_ids = sorted(sites)
    customer_ids = sorted(customers)
    lane_ids = sorted(lanes)
    scenario_ids = sorted(scenarios)

    probability_sum = sum(scenarios[w]["probability"] for w in scenario_ids)
    if abs(probability_sum - 1.0) > 1e-8:
        raise ValueError(f"Scenario probabilities sum to {probability_sum}, not 1")

    high_purity_customers = {
        c for c in customer_ids if customers[c]["purity_required_pct"] >= HIGH_PURITY_THRESHOLD
    }

    lanes_by_site = defaultdict(list)
    lanes_by_customer = defaultdict(list)
    lanes_by_site_high = defaultdict(list)
    lanes_by_site_low = defaultdict(list)
    for lane_id, lane in lanes.items():
        lanes_by_site[lane["origin_site"]].append(lane_id)
        lanes_by_customer[lane["customer_id"]].append(lane_id)
        if lane["customer_id"] in high_purity_customers:
            lanes_by_site_high[lane["origin_site"]].append(lane_id)
        else:
            lanes_by_site_low[lane["origin_site"]].append(lane_id)

    max_polish = {}
    for site_id, site in sites.items():
        max_polish[site_id] = site["initial_inventory_kg"] + len(hours) * site["max_modules"] * site["module_capacity_kgph"]

    return {
        "config": config,
        "tol": tol,
        "hours": hours,
        "sites": sites,
        "customers": customers,
        "lanes": lanes,
        "scenarios": scenarios,
        "forecast": forecast,
        "scenario_site": scenario_site,
        "scenario_demand": scenario_demand,
        "scenario_lane": scenario_lane,
        "base_demand": base_demand,
        "daily_demand": dict(daily_demand),
        "site_ids": site_ids,
        "customer_ids": customer_ids,
        "lane_ids": lane_ids,
        "scenario_ids": scenario_ids,
        "high_purity_customers": high_purity_customers,
        "lanes_by_site": dict(lanes_by_site),
        "lanes_by_customer": dict(lanes_by_customer),
        "lanes_by_site_high": dict(lanes_by_site_high),
        "lanes_by_site_low": dict(lanes_by_site_low),
        "water_multiplier": water_multiplier,
        "max_polish": max_polish,
    }


def build_model(data, time_limit):
    config = data["config"]

    customer_ids = data["customer_ids"]

    forecast = data["forecast"]
    scenario_site = data["scenario_site"]
    scenario_demand = data["scenario_demand"]
    scenario_lane = data["scenario_lane"]

    model = Model("green_hydrogen_hub_resilience")
    model.setParam("limits/gap", GAP_LIMIT)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)

    active = {}
    modules = {}

/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '521,860p' /app/submissions/solve.py"

    model.setObjective(infra_cost + expected_cost + config["tail_risk_weight"] * cvar_cost, "minimize")

def extract_solution(data, model, vars_dict):
    tol = data["tol"]
    hours = data["hours"]
    site_ids = data["site_ids"]
    scenario_ids = data["scenario_ids"]
    lane_ids = data["lane_ids"]
    customer_ids = data["customer_ids"]
    sol = model.getBestSol()

    infrastructure = []
    for site_id in site_ids:
        infrastructure.append(
            {
                "site_id": site_id,
                "active": bool(round(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["active"][site_id]))),
                "electrolyzer_modules": clean_int(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["modules"][site_id])),
                "tank_modules": clean_int(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["tank_modules"][site_id])),
                "polishing_active": bool(round(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["polishing_active"][site_id]))),
            }
        )

    operations = []
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for hour in hours:
            for site_id in site_ids:
                key = (scenario_id, site_id, hour)
                production_value = model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["production"][key])
                storage_value = model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["unpolished_inventory"][key]) + model.getSolVal(
                    sol, vars_dict["polished_inventory"][key]
                )
                vent_value = model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["vent_unpolished"][key]) + model.getSolVal(
                    sol, vars_dict["vent_polished"][key]
                )
                operations.append(
                    {
                        "scenario_id": scenario_id,
                        "hour": hour,
                        "site_id": site_id,
                        "online_modules": clean_int(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["online"][key])),
                        "startup_modules": clean_int(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["startup"][key])),
                        "production_kg": clean_float(production_value),
                        "renewable_mwh": clean_float(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["renewable"][key])),
                        "grid_mwh": clean_float(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["grid"][key])),
                        "storage_end_kg": clean_float(storage_value),
                        "vent_kg": clean_float(vent_value),
                        "water_m3": clean_float(data["config"]["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * production_value),
                        "polishing_kg": clean_float(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["polished"][key])),
                    }
                )

    deliveries = []
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for hour in hours:
            for lane_id in lane_ids:
                key = (scenario_id, lane_id, hour)
                trip_value = model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["trips"][key])
                kg_value = model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["delivered"][key])
                if abs(trip_value) <= tol and abs(kg_value) <= tol:
                    continue
                deliveries.append(
                    {
                        "scenario_id": scenario_id,
                        "hour": hour,
                        "lane_id": lane_id,
                        "kg": clean_float(kg_value),
                        "trips": clean_int(trip_value),
                    }
                )

    shortfalls = []
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for hour in hours:
            for customer_id in customer_ids:
                key = (scenario_id, customer_id, hour)
                shortfalls.append(
                    {
                        "scenario_id": scenario_id,
                        "hour": hour,
                        "customer_id": customer_id,
                        "kg": clean_float(model.getSolVal(sol, vars_dict["shortfall"][key])),
                    }
                )

    return {
        "infrastructure": infrastructure,
        "operations": operations,
        "deliveries": deliveries,
        "shortfalls": shortfalls,
    }


def compute_cvar(prob_cost_pairs, alpha):
    best = math.inf
    best_eta = None
    for eta in sorted({cost for _, cost in prob_cost_pairs}):
        value = eta + sum(prob * max(cost - eta, 0.0) for prob, cost in prob_cost_pairs) / (1.0 - alpha)
        if value < best:
            best = value
            best_eta = eta
    return best, best_eta


def validate_solution(data, solution):
    tol = data["tol"]
    hours = data["hours"]
    site_ids = data["site_ids"]
    customer_ids = data["customer_ids"]
    scenario_ids = data["scenario_ids"]
    sites = data["sites"]
    customers = data["customers"]
    lanes = data["lanes"]
    scenarios = data["scenarios"]
    forecast = data["forecast"]
    scenario_site = data["scenario_site"]
    scenario_demand = data["scenario_demand"]
    scenario_lane = data["scenario_lane"]
    config = data["config"]

    issues = []
    group_residuals = defaultdict(float)
    integrality_residual = 0.0

    def record(group, residual, scale, message):
        normalized = abs(residual) / max(1.0, scale)
        group_residuals[group] = max(group_residuals[group], normalized)
        if normalized > tol:
            issues.append(f"{group}: {message} (residual={residual:.8g}, normalized={normalized:.8g})")

    def parse_bool(value):
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return value
        if value in (0, 1):
            return bool(value)
        raise ValueError(f"Expected boolean, got {value!r}")

    if set(solution.keys()) != {"infrastructure", "operations", "deliveries", "shortfalls"}:
        issues.append(f"top-level keys mismatch: {sorted(solution.keys())}")

    infra = {}
    if len(solution.get("infrastructure", [])) != len(site_ids):
        issues.append("infrastructure row count mismatch")
    for row in solution.get("infrastructure", []):
        site_id = row.get("site_id")
        if site_id not in sites or site_id in infra:
            issues.append(f"invalid infrastructure row {site_id}")
            continue
        modules = row.get("electrolyzer_modules")
        tanks = row.get("tank_modules")
        if abs(round(modules) - modules) > 1e-8:
            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(modules) - modules))
        if abs(round(tanks) - tanks) > 1e-8:
            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(tanks) - tanks))
        active = parse_bool(row.get("active"))
        polish = parse_bool(row.get("polishing_active"))
        modules = int(round(modules))
        tanks = int(round(tanks))
        infra[site_id] = {
            "active": active,
            "modules": modules,
            "tanks": tanks,
            "polishing_active": polish,
        }
        record("infra_modules", max(0.0, modules - sites[site_id]["max_modules"]), sites[site_id]["max_modules"], site_id)
        record("infra_tanks", max(0.0, tanks - sites[site_id]["max_tank_modules"]), sites[site_id]["max_tank_modules"], site_id)
        if not active:
            record("infra_active", modules + tanks + (1 if polish else 0), 1.0, site_id)

    for site_id in site_ids:
        infra.setdefault(site_id, {"active": False, "modules": 0, "tanks": 0, "polishing_active": False})

    ops = {}
    expected_ops = len(scenario_ids) * len(hours) * len(site_ids)
    if len(solution.get("operations", [])) != expected_ops:
        issues.append("operations row count mismatch")
    for row in solution.get("operations", []):
        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("site_id"))
        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in sites or key in ops:
            issues.append(f"invalid operations row {key}")
            continue
        online = row.get("online_modules")
        startup = row.get("startup_modules")
        if abs(round(online) - online) > 1e-8:
            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(online) - online))
        if abs(round(startup) - startup) > 1e-8:
            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(startup) - startup))
        ops[key] = {
            "online": int(round(online)),
            "startup": int(round(startup)),
            "production": float(row.get("production_kg")),
            "renewable": float(row.get("renewable_mwh")),
            "grid": float(row.get("grid_mwh")),
            "storage": float(row.get("storage_end_kg")),
            "vent": float(row.get("vent_kg")),
            "water": float(row.get("water_m3")),
            "polishing": float(row.get("polishing_kg")),
        }
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for hour in hours:
            for site_id in site_ids:
                ops.setdefault(
                    (scenario_id, hour, site_id),
                    {"online": 0, "startup": 0, "production": 0.0, "renewable": 0.0, "grid": 0.0, "storage": 0.0, "vent": 0.0, "water": 0.0, "polishing": 0.0},
                )

    deliveries = {}
    inbound = defaultdict(float)
    outbound = defaultdict(float)
    outbound_low = defaultdict(float)
    outbound_high = defaultdict(float)
    driver_hours = defaultdict(float)
    for row in solution.get("deliveries", []):
        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("lane_id"))
        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in lanes or key in deliveries:
            issues.append(f"invalid delivery row {key}")
            continue
        trips = row.get("trips")
        if abs(round(trips) - trips) > 1e-8:
            integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(round(trips) - trips))
        trips = int(round(trips))
        kg = float(row.get("kg"))
        deliveries[key] = {"trips": trips, "kg": kg}
        lane = lanes[key[2]]
        inbound[(key[0], key[1], lane["customer_id"])] += kg
        outbound[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
        if lane["customer_id"] in data["high_purity_customers"]:
            outbound_high[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
        else:
            outbound_low[(key[0], key[1], lane["origin_site"])] += kg
        sl = scenario_lane[(key[0], key[2], key[1])]
        driver_hours[(key[0], key[1])] += trips * lane["driver_hours_per_trip"] * sl["travel_time_multiplier"]
        record("trip_cap", max(0.0, trips - sl["max_trips"]), max(1.0, sl["max_trips"]), str(key))
        record("payload", max(0.0, kg - lane["kg_per_trip"] * trips), max(1.0, lane["kg_per_trip"]), str(key))
        if not infra[lane["origin_site"]]["active"]:
            record("inactive_delivery", trips + kg, 1.0, str(key))

    shorts = {}
    expected_shorts = len(scenario_ids) * len(hours) * len(customer_ids)
    if len(solution.get("shortfalls", [])) != expected_shorts:
        issues.append("shortfalls row count mismatch")
    for row in solution.get("shortfalls", []):
        key = (row.get("scenario_id"), row.get("hour"), row.get("customer_id"))
        if key[0] not in scenarios or key[1] not in hours or key[2] not in customers or key in shorts:
            issues.append(f"invalid shortfall row {key}")
            continue
        shorts[key] = float(row.get("kg"))
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        for hour in hours:
            for customer_id in customer_ids:
                shorts.setdefault((scenario_id, hour, customer_id), 0.0)

    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        scenario = scenarios[scenario_id]
        emissions = 0.0
        for hour in hours:
            record(
                "driver_hours",
                max(0.0, driver_hours[(scenario_id, hour)] - scenario["driver_hours_cap"]),
                max(1.0, scenario["driver_hours_cap"]),
                f"{scenario_id},{hour}",
            )
            reserve = sum(ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]["storage"] for site_id in site_ids)
            record(
                "reserve",
                max(0.0, config["system_min_reserve_kg"] - reserve),
                max(1.0, config["system_min_reserve_kg"]),
                f"{scenario_id},{hour}",
            )

        for customer_id in customer_ids:
            customer = customers[customer_id]
            total_short = 0.0
            for hour in hours:
                demand = scenario_demand[(scenario_id, customer_id, hour)]
                delivered = inbound[(scenario_id, hour, customer_id)]
                short = shorts[(scenario_id, hour, customer_id)]
                total_short += short
                record("demand_balance", delivered + short - demand, max(1.0, demand), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{customer_id}")
                record(
                    "hourly_shortfall",
                    max(0.0, short - customer["max_hourly_shortfall_fraction"] * demand),
                    max(1.0, demand),
                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{customer_id}",
                )
            record(
                "service_floor",
                max(0.0, total_short - (1.0 - customer["service_floor_fraction"]) * data["daily_demand"][(scenario_id, customer_id)]),
                max(1.0, data["daily_demand"][(scenario_id, customer_id)]),
                f"{scenario_id},{customer_id}",
            )

        for site_id in site_ids:
            site = sites[site_id]
            storage_cap = site["tank_base_kg"] + site["tank_module_capacity_kg"] * infra[site_id]["tanks"]
            water_total = 0.0
            startup_total = 0.0
            p_lo = 0.0
            p_hi = 0.0
            for hour in hours:
                row = ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]
                sh = scenario_site[(scenario_id, site_id, hour)]
                fc = forecast[(site_id, hour)]
                cap = site["module_capacity_kgph"] * sh["module_capacity_multiplier"]
                prev_storage = site["initial_inventory_kg"] if hour == 0 else ops[(scenario_id, hour - 1, site_id)]["storage"]
                prev_online = 0 if hour == 0 else ops[(scenario_id, hour - 1, site_id)]["online"]

                record("online", max(0.0, row["online"] - infra[site_id]["modules"]), max(1.0, infra[site_id]["modules"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                if not infra[site_id]["active"]:
                    record(
                        "inactive_ops",
                        row["online"] + row["startup"] + row["production"] + row["renewable"] + row["grid"] + row["vent"] + row["polishing"],
                        1.0,
                        f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
                    )
                record("prod_upper", max(0.0, row["production"] - cap * row["online"]), max(1.0, cap), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                record(
                    "prod_lower",
                    max(0.0, site["min_load_fraction"] * cap * row["online"] - row["production"]),
tool8. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '861,1140p' /app/submissions/solve.py"
result9. Command output
                    max(1.0, cap),
                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
                )
                record(
                    "energy",
                    max(0.0, config["energy_mwh_per_kg_h2"] * row["production"] + config["polishing_mwh_per_kg"] * row["polishing"] - row["renewable"] - row["grid"]),
                    max(1.0, row["renewable"] + row["grid"]),
                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
                )
                record("renewable_cap", max(0.0, row["renewable"] - fc["renewable_mwh"] * sh["renewable_multiplier"]), max(1.0, fc["renewable_mwh"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                record("grid_cap", max(0.0, row["grid"] - sh["grid_cap_mwh"]), max(1.0, sh["grid_cap_mwh"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                record("water_link", row["water"] - config["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * row["production"], max(1.0, row["water"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                record(
                    "polishing_active",
                    max(0.0, row["polishing"] - data["max_polish"][site_id] * (1 if infra[site_id]["polishing_active"] else 0)),
                    max(1.0, data["max_polish"][site_id]),
                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
                )
                record("storage_cap", max(0.0, row["storage"] - storage_cap), max(1.0, storage_cap), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")
                record(
                    "inventory_balance",
                    row["storage"] - prev_storage - row["production"] + outbound[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] + row["vent"],
                    max(1.0, prev_storage + row["production"]),
                    f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}",
                )
                record("startup_delta", max(0.0, row["online"] - prev_online - row["startup"]), max(1.0, row["online"]), f"{scenario_id},{hour},{site_id}")

                p_lo = max(0.0, p_lo + row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] - row["vent"])
                p_hi = min(row["storage"], p_hi + row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)])
                if hour == 0:
                    p_lo = max(0.0, row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)] - row["vent"])
                    p_hi = min(row["storage"], row["polishing"] - outbound_high[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)])
                if p_lo > p_hi + tol:
                    issues.append(f"purity_balance: {scenario_id},{hour},{site_id} has no feasible polished inventory split")
                    group_residuals["purity_balance"] = max(group_residuals["purity_balance"], p_lo - p_hi)

                water_total += row["water"]
                startup_total += row["startup"]
                emissions += fc["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"] * row["grid"]

            record("startup_limit", max(0.0, startup_total - site["startup_limit"]), max(1.0, site["startup_limit"]), f"{scenario_id},{site_id}")
            record(
                "water_limit",
                max(0.0, water_total - site["water_limit_m3_per_day"] * data["water_multiplier"][(scenario_id, site_id)]),
                max(1.0, site["water_limit_m3_per_day"]),
                f"{scenario_id},{site_id}",
            )
            record(
                "final_inventory",
                max(0.0, site["final_min_inventory_kg"] - ops[(scenario_id, hours[-1], site_id)]["storage"]),
                max(1.0, site["final_min_inventory_kg"]),
                f"{scenario_id},{site_id}",
            )

        record("emission_cap", max(0.0, emissions - scenario["emission_cap_kg"]), max(1.0, scenario["emission_cap_kg"]), scenario_id)

    infra_cost = sum(
        sites[site_id]["module_daily_cost"] * infra[site_id]["modules"]
        + sites[site_id]["tank_daily_cost"] * infra[site_id]["tanks"]
        + sites[site_id]["polishing_daily_cost"] * (1 if infra[site_id]["polishing_active"] else 0)
        for site_id in site_ids
    )
    op_costs = {}
    for scenario_id in scenario_ids:
        total = 0.0
        for site_id in site_ids:
            for hour in hours:
                row = ops[(scenario_id, hour, site_id)]
                fc = forecast[(site_id, hour)]
                total += sites[site_id]["variable_cost_per_kg"] * row["production"]
                total += config["water_cost_per_m3"] * config["water_m3_per_kg_h2"] * row["production"]
                total += config["startup_cost_per_module"] * row["startup"]
                total += sites[site_id]["polishing_cost_per_kg"] * row["polishing"]
                total += config["vent_penalty_per_kg"] * row["vent"]
                total += scenarios[scenario_id]["grid_price_multiplier"] * fc["grid_price_per_mwh"] * row["grid"]
                total += config["emission_cost_per_kg"] * fc["grid_emission_kg_per_mwh"] * row["grid"]
        for (scen, _, lane_id), row in deliveries.items():
            if scen == scenario_id:
                total += lanes[lane_id]["cost_per_trip"] * row["trips"]
        for (scen, _, customer_id), short in shorts.items():
            if scen == scenario_id:
                total += customers[customer_id]["shortfall_cost_per_kg"] * customers[customer_id]["priority_weight"] * short
        op_costs[scenario_id] = total

    expected_cost = sum(scenarios[w]["probability"] * op_costs[w] for w in scenario_ids)
    cvar_value, cvar_eta = compute_cvar([(scenarios[w]["probability"], op_costs[w]) for w in scenario_ids], config["tail_risk_alpha"])
    objective = infra_cost + expected_cost + config["tail_risk_weight"] * cvar_value

    return {
        "valid": not issues,
        "issues": issues,
        "max_normalized_residual": max(group_residuals.values(), default=0.0),
        "violated_constraint_groups": sum(1 for value in group_residuals.values() if value > tol),
        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
        "group_residuals": dict(sorted(group_residuals.items())),
        "coverage": {
            "infrastructure_rows": len(solution.get("infrastructure", [])),
            "operations_rows": len(solution.get("operations", [])),
            "shortfall_rows": len(solution.get("shortfalls", [])),
            "delivery_rows": len(solution.get("deliveries", [])),
        },
        "infrastructure_cost": infra_cost,
        "scenario_operating_costs": op_costs,
        "expected_operating_cost": expected_cost,
        "cvar_value": cvar_value,
        "cvar_eta": cvar_eta,
        "objective": objective,
    }


def run_negative_audit(data, solution):
    corrupted = copy.deepcopy(solution)
    if corrupted["shortfalls"]:
        corrupted["shortfalls"][0]["kg"] = clean_float(corrupted["shortfalls"][0]["kg"] + 1.0)
    elif corrupted["operations"]:
        corrupted["operations"][0]["storage_end_kg"] = clean_float(corrupted["operations"][0]["storage_end_kg"] + 1.0)
    audit = validate_solution(data, corrupted)
    return not audit["valid"]


def write_solution(solution):
    SOLUTION_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(solution, indent=2) + "\n")


def safe_gap(model):
    try:
        return model.getGap()
    except Exception:
        return None


def safe_obj(model):
    try:
        return model.getObjVal()
    except Exception:
        return None


def safe_bound(model):
    try:
        return model.getDualbound()
    except Exception:
        return None


def write_log(command, time_limit, elapsed, model, validation, negative_audit_ok):
    status = str(model.getStatus())
    nsols = model.getNSols()
    obj = safe_obj(model)
    bound = safe_bound(model)
    gap = safe_gap(model)
    lines = [
        "# Solve Log",
        "",
        "## Commands",
        "",
        f"- `PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python /app/submissions/solve.py`",
        "",
        "## Solver",
        "",
        f"- Status: `{status}`",
        f"- Solutions found: `{nsols}`",
        f"- Time limit used: `{time_limit}` seconds",
        f"- Elapsed solve+audit time: `{elapsed:.3f}` seconds",
    ]
    if obj is not None:
        lines.append(f"- Solver objective: `{obj:.8f}`")
    if bound is not None:
        lines.append(f"- Best bound: `{bound:.8f}`")
    if gap is not None:
        lines.append(f"- Relative gap: `{gap:.8f}`")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "## Validation",
            "",
            f"- Validation passed: `{validation['valid']}`",
            f"- Independent objective recomputation: `{validation['objective']:.8f}`",
            f"- Max normalized residual: `{validation['max_normalized_residual']:.8g}`",
            f"- Violated constraint groups: `{validation['violated_constraint_groups']}`",
            f"- Integrality residual: `{validation['integrality_residual']:.8g}`",
            f"- Negative audit rejected corrupted copy: `{negative_audit_ok}`",
            f"- Coverage: `{validation['coverage']}`",
            f"- Infrastructure cost: `{validation['infrastructure_cost']:.8f}`",
            f"- Expected operating cost: `{validation['expected_operating_cost']:.8f}`",
            f"- CVaR value: `{validation['cvar_value']:.8f}`",
        ]
    )

    if validation["scenario_operating_costs"]:
        lines.extend(["", "## Scenario Operating Costs", ""])
        for scenario_id, value in sorted(validation["scenario_operating_costs"].items()):
            lines.append(f"- `{scenario_id}`: `{value:.8f}`")

    lines.extend(["", "## Residuals By Group", ""])
    for group, value in validation["group_residuals"].items():
        lines.append(f"- `{group}`: `{value:.8g}`")

    lines.extend(["", "## Validation Notes", ""])
    if validation["issues"]:
        for issue in validation["issues"][:50]:
            lines.append(f"- {issue}")
    else:
        lines.append("- No validation issues detected.")

    LOG_PATH.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")


def main():
    start = time.time()
    time_limit = parse_time_limit()
    data = build_data()
    model, vars_dict = build_model(data, time_limit)
    model.optimize()

    if model.getNSols() <= 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"SCIP ended with status {model.getStatus()} and no incumbent solution")

    solution = extract_solution(data, model, vars_dict)
    write_solution(solution)
    validation = validate_solution(data, solution)
    if not validation["valid"]:
        raise RuntimeError("Exported solution failed independent validation")

    negative_audit_ok = run_negative_audit(data, solution)
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    write_log(sys.argv, time_limit, elapsed, model, validation, negative_audit_ok)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

        Create a new primal solution in the transformed space.

        Parameters
        ----------
        heur : Heur or None, optional
            heuristic that found the solution (Default value = None)
        initlp : bool, optional
            Should the created solution be initialised to the current LP solution instead of all zeros

        Returns
        -------
        Solution



 setSolVal

        Set a variable in a solution.

        Parameters
        ----------
        solution : Solution
            solution to be modified
        var : Variable
            variable in the solution
        val : float
            value of the specified variable



 addSol

        Try to add a solution to the storage.

        Parameters
        ----------
        solution : Solution
            solution to store
        free : bool, optional
            should solution be freed afterwards? (Default value = True)

        Returns
        -------
        stored : bool
            stores whether given solution was good enough to keep



 trySol

        Check given primal solution for feasibility and try to add it to the storage.

        Parameters
        ----------
        solution : Solution
            solution to store
        printreason : bool, optional
            should all reasons of violations be printed? (Default value = True)
        completely : bool, optional
            should all violation be checked? (Default value = False)
        checkbounds : bool, optional
            should the bounds of the variables be checked? (Default value = True)
        checkintegrality : bool, optional
            does integrality have to be checked? (Default value = True)
        checklprows : bool, optional
            do current LP rows (both local and global) have to be checked? (Default value = True)
        free : bool, optional
            should solution be freed? (Default value = True)

        Returns
        -------
        stored : bool
            whether given solution was feasible and good enough to keep
